Personal information provided on this form is collected and will be used, disclosed, and retained by Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship Canada (IRCC) under the authority of the Immigration and Refugee Protection Act (IRPA). The personal information provided will be used for the purpose of processing applications. The personal information provided may be disclosed to other federal government institutions and third parties including law enforcement bodies, provincial/territorial governments and/or foreign governments for the purpose of validating identity, eligibility and admissibility. The personal information collected on an application, and other information collected in support of an application, may be used for advanced analytics, automation, and other technologies to support processing of applications and decision making, including your application. Personal information, including from advanced analytics, automation, and other technologies, may also be used for purposes including research, statistics, program and policy evaluation, internal audit, compliance, risk management, strategy development and reporting. Where biometrics are provided in support of an application, the fingerprints collected will be stored and shared with the RCMP. The fingerprint record may also be disclosed to law enforcement agencies in Canada in accordance with subsection 13.11(1) of the Immigration and Refugee Protection Regulations. The information may be used to establish or verify the identity of a person in order to prevent, investigate, or prosecute an offence under any law of Canada or a Province. This information may also be used to establish or verify the identity of an individual whose identity cannot reasonably be otherwise established or verified because of physical or mental condition. Canada may also share immigration information related to biometric records with foreign governments with whom Canada has an agreement or arrangement. Failure to complete the form in full may result in a delay or the application not being processed. The Privacy Act gives individuals the right of access to, protection, and correction of their personal information. Further details are available in Info Source . If you are not satisfied with the manner in which IRCC handles your personal information, you may exercise your right to file a complaint to the Office of the Privacy Commissioner of Canada . The collection, use, disclosure and retention of your personal information is further described in IRCC’s personal information bank – IRCC PPU 068. bcField.caption.value.text.value; if (includeLabel == true && barcodeLabel.length > 0) { fieldValues.push(barcodeLabel); } if(collection != null) { var oNode = collection; var count = oNode.nodes.length; for(var i = 0; i <= count - 1; ++i){ var oNodeItem = oNode.nodes.item(i); var realNode = xfa.resolveNode(oNodeItem.value); if (realNode != null){ fieldValues.push(realNode.value); } else fieldValues.push(""); } } var encodedContent = ""; var values = fieldValues.join(delimiter); values += delimiter; if(includeFieldNames == true) { var fields = fieldNames.join(delimiter); fields += delimiter; encodedContent = fields + "\n" + values; } else { encodedContent = values; } return encodedContent; } function hideBarcodes() { if (form1.Barcodes.presence == "visible"){ form1.Barcodes.presence = "hidden"; //form1.Page3.Consent1.presence = "visible"; } } function showBarcodes() { if (form1.Barcodes.presence == "hidden"){ form1.Barcodes.presence = "visible"; oNode = xfa.datasets.resolveNode("LOVFile"); if(oNode == null) { oNode = xfa.datasets.createNode("dataGroup", "LOVFile"); xfa.datasets.nodes.append(oNode); } if(oNode != null) { USE FOR CHILD LOV of HIERARCHICAL. FORM SIMPLE LOV USE DYNAMIC DROPDOWN * * oNode - the DDL object * listPath - the path of the list, relative to xfa.datasets.LOVFile.LOV * * BZAHER 11/24/09 * CPARK 8/18/10 modified code to handle null *******************************************************************************/ function setDDLFromLOV(oNode, listPath) { try { var someIndex; if(listPath == "") { oNode.clearItems(); oNode.selectedIndex = -1; // clear the selection // colour the field to indicate that it is inactive oNode.ui.resolveNode("#choiceList").border.fill.color.value = "230,230,230"; } else { var oNodeList = xfa.resolveNodes("xfa.datasets.LOVFile.LOV." + listPath); var itemString = ""; var oldValue = oNode.rawValue; var oldValueFound = false; // colour the field white, since there's data //oNode.ui.resolveNode("#choiceList").border.fill.color.value = "255,255,255"; // remove the existing items oNode.clearItems(); // add the new items for(var i = 0; i < oNodeList.length; i++) { var oListItem = oNodeList.item(i); var key = String(oListItem.lic.value); // NEW 8/18/10 - add check to handle null if (oListItem.value == null) oListItem.value = " "; oNode.addItem(oListItem.value, key); if(oldValue == key) { oldValueFound = true; someIndex = i; } } // decide whether we need to maintain the selected value, or clear it if(oldValueFound == false) { oNode.selectedIndex = -1; // clear the selection } else{ oNode.selectedIndex = someIndex; // maintian the selectin } } } catch(e) { console.show(); console.println(e.toString()); throw e; } finally { oNodeList = null; oListItem = null; } } /********************************************************************************* * Validate UCI, service language, visaType * Chan Chac march 11, 2011 **********************************************************************************/ function validateFirstSection() { try { var oUCI = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.UCIClientID"); var oServiceIn = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.ServiceIn.ServiceIn"); var oVisaType = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.VisaType.VisaType"); processField(oUCI, validateUCI, oUCI); processField(oServiceIn, validateDropDown, oServiceIn); processField(oVisaType, validateDropDown, oVisaType); } catch(e) { console.show(); console.println(e.toString()); throw e; } finally { oUCI = null; oServiceIn = null; oVisaType = null; } } /********************************************************************************* * Validate Family Name, Given Name, Alias Indicator, Alias Fam Name, & * Alias Given Name * Chan Chac march 11, 2011 **********************************************************************************/ function validateNames() { try { var oFamilyName = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.Name.FamilyName"); var oGivenName = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.Name.GivenName"); var oAliasIndicator = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.AliasName.AliasNameIndicator.AliasNameIndicator"); var oAliasFamilyName= xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.AliasName.AliasFamilyName"); var oAliasGivenName = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.AliasName.AliasGivenName"); processField(oFamilyName, validateName, oFamilyName); processField(oGivenName, validateName, oGivenName); ////////////////////////Validate Alias Indicator////////////////////////////////////// //Negative Cases for drop down //if No is checked and Family Name or Given name has something, throw an error if ((oAliasIndicator.rawValue == "N" && (!oAliasFamilyName.isNull || !oAliasGivenName.isNull))){ if (globFirstField.value == "") xfa.host.messageBox(messages.getMessage("negative_case")); oAliasIndicator.No.rawValue = null; oAliasIndicator.Yes.rawValue = null; xfa.host.setFocus(oAliasIndicator.No); setGlobFirstField(oAliasIndicator.No); } //State check: if No is checked, and Fam Name is empty, and Given is Empty, and Acess is Open, then disable Family name and Given name if ((oAliasIndicator.rawValue == "N" && oAliasFamilyName.isNull && oAliasGivenName.isNull && oAliasFamilyName.access == "open" )){ color.markCorrect(oAliasGivenName); color.markCorrect(oAliasFamilyName); color.markUnRequired(oAliasIndicator.No); color.markUnRequired(oAliasIndicator.Yes); color.markUnRequired(oAliasFamilyName); util.enableField(oAliasFamilyName.somExpression, false); util.enableField(oAliasGivenName.somExpression, false); } //if Yes is checked, and Access != "open", then enable Family name and Given name if ((oAliasIndicator.rawValue == "Y" && oAliasFamilyName.access != "open")){ color.markCorrect(oAliasGivenName); color.markCorrect(oAliasFamilyName); color.markUnRequired(oAliasIndicator.No); color.markUnRequired(oAliasIndicator.Yes); util.enableField(oAliasFamilyName.somExpression, true); util.enableField(oAliasGivenName.somExpression, true); color.markRequired(oAliasFamilyName); } //validate drop down if(util.isRequiredAndEmpty(oAliasIndicator)) { setGlobFirstField(oAliasIndicator.No); color.markCorrect(oAliasIndicator.No); color.markCorrect(oAliasIndicator.Yes); color.markRequired(oAliasIndicator.No); color.markRequired(oAliasIndicator.Yes); xfa.host.setFocus(oAliasIndicator.No); } ///Validate Alias Family Name field processField(oAliasFamilyName, validateName, oAliasFamilyName); processField(oAliasGivenName, validateName, oAliasGivenName); processField(oAliasFamilyName, validateDuplicateName, oGivenName, oFamilyName, oAliasGivenName, oAliasFamilyName); } catch(e) { console.show(); console.println(e.toString()); throw e; } finally { oFamilyName = null; oGivenName = null; oAliasIndicator = null; oAliasFamilyName= null; oAliasGivenName = null; } } /********************************************************************************* * Validate Gender, DOB, POB, Citizenship * Chan Chac march 11, 2011 **********************************************************************************/ function validateGenderSection() { try { var oGender = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.Sex.Sex"); var oYYYY = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.DOBYear"); var oMM = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.DOBMonth"); var oDD = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.DOBDay"); var oCity = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.PlaceBirthCity"); var oCountry = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.PlaceBirthCountry"); var oCitizenship = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.Citizenship.Citizenship"); var oAge = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.ButtonsHeader.Age"); //Validate Gender field processField(oGender, validateDropDown, oGender); //Validate DOB YYYY fields if (oYYYY.isNull) { //null clear age oAge.rawValue = null; oAge.execEvent("exit"); if (oYYYY.mandatory == "error"){ color.markRequired(oYYYY); setGlobFirstField(oYYYY); } } else { val.validateDOBYYYY(oYYYY, oAge); } //Validate DOB MM fields if (oMM.isNull) { //null clear age oAge.rawValue = null; oAge.execEvent("exit"); if (oMM.mandatory == "error"){ color.markRequired(oMM); setGlobFirstField(oMM); } } else { val.validateDOBMM(oMM, oAge); } //Validate DOB DD fields if (oDD.isNull) { //null clear age oAge.rawValue = null; oAge.execEvent("exit"); if (oDD.mandatory == "error"){ color.markRequired(oDD); setGlobFirstField(oDD); } } else { val.validateDOBDD(oYYYY, oMM, oDD, oAge); } //POB City processField(oCity, validatePOBText, oCity); //Validate Country field processField(oCountry, validateDropDown, oCountry); //Validate Citizenship field processField(oCitizenship, validateDropDown, oCitizenship); } catch(e) { console.show(); console.println(e.toString()); throw e; } finally { oGender = null; oYYYY = null; oMM = null; oDD = null; oCity = null; oCountry = null; oCitizenship = null; oAge = null; } } /********************************************************************************* * Validates COR section: Country, Status, Other, From, TO * Chan Chac march 14, 2011 **********************************************************************************/ function validateCOR(){ try { var oCountry = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.CurrentCOR.Row2.Country"); var oStatus = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.CurrentCOR.Row2.Status"); var oOther = xfa.resolveNode("Page1.PersonalDetails.CurrentCOR.Row2.Other"); var oFrom = } else if (oField.ui.oneOfChild.className == "checkButton") { // or check box HAVE NOT PARENT var vm = oField.validationMessage.split("\r") //CPARK 1/23 72368 xfa.host.messageBox(vm[0] + "\n\n" + messages.getMessage("Required")+ "\n\n" + vm[1], messages.getMessage("Validation Error")); } //field else xfa.host.messageBox(oField.formatMessage + "\n\n" + messages.getMessage("Required")+"\n\n" + oField.validationMessage, messages.getMessage("Validation Error")); }   //function to show the message of the first required or incorrect field //this function is the only place where it can clear the first field value as well.   function popMessage(){ var result = false; if(globFirstField.value != ""){ var oNode = xfa.resolveNode(globFirstField.value); if(oNode != null){ xfa.host.setFocus(oNode ); if (util.isRequiredAndEmpty(oNode)) showRequiredMessage(oNode); util.colorCheckBoxes(oNode); } } else{ result = true; } return result; }   //function to set the Global Variable 'globFirstField' to hold the location of the //first found required field or invalid field   function setGlobFirstField(oField){ if (globFirstField.value == "") globFirstField.value = oField.somExpression; } /********************************************************************************* *Function: processField *Parameters: Field object, Pointer to a Function, optional args for the function (0....n times). *Returns: pointer to an Array where array[0] = boolean, array[1] = error message *Description: * - checks if a field is required and empty * - if so, colors it red, sets it in the global variable * - if not, executes the function passed in the parameter func, and its arguments found in args * - the switch statement is necessary in order to determine the number of arguments to pass. *Chan Chac march 11, 2011 **********************************************************************************/ function processField(oField, func /*0....n args*/){ var result = new Array(); result[0] = true; var args = Array.prototype.slice.call(arguments).splice(2); if (util.isRequiredAndEmpty(oField)){ color.markRequired(oField); setGlobFirstField(oField) result[0] = false; } else{ if (globFirstField.value == ""){ //put switch statement here switch(args.length){ case 0: result = func(); break; case 1: result = func(args[0]); break; case 2: result = func(args[0], args[1]); break; case 3: result = func(args[0], args[1], args[2]); break; case 4: result = func(args[0], args[1], args[2], args[3]); break; case 5: result = func(args[0], args[1], args[2], args[3], args[4]); break; case 6: result = func(args[0], args[1], args[2], args[3], args[4], args[5]); break; default: } if (!result[0]) setGlobFirstField(oField); } } return result; } ////////////////////////////functions to return functions in another script object///////////////////////////////////////////////////// // since JavaScript functions are not polymorphic, we must write wrapper functions here to return functions in another script object.// // this is necessary because designer has trouble returning functions when using partial function pointers that point to code in // // another script object. //   function validateUCI(oUCI){ return val.validateUCI(oUCI); } function validateDropDown(oField){ return val.validateDropDown(oField); } function validateName(oField){ return val.validateName(oField); } function validateDateWithHiddenFields(oDateA, sOperator, sDateB, oYYYY, oMM, oDD){ return val.validateDateWithHiddenFields(oDateA, sOperator, sDateB, oYYYY, oMM, oDD); } function validateDate(oDateA, sOperator, sDateB){ return val.validateDate(oDateA, sOperator, sDateB); } function validateOpenText(oField){ return val.validateOpenText(oField); } function validateDateNID(oDateA, sOperator, sDateB){ return val.validateDateNID(oDateA, sOperator, sDateB); } function validatePassportNum(oField){ return val.validatePassportNum(oField); } function validatePostalCodeBasedOnCountry(oPostalCode, oCountry){ return val.validatePostalCodeBasedOnCountry(oPostalCode, oCountry); } function validateCountryTeleCode(oCountryCode){ return val.validateCountryTeleCode(oCountryCode); } function validateDigits(oPhone){ return val.validateDigits(oPhone); } function validateAreaCode(oPhone){ return val.validateAreaCode(oPhone); } function validatePhoneNAN(oPhone){ return val.validatePhoneNAN(oPhone); } function validateEmailField(oEmail){ return val.validateEmail(oEmail); } function validateFunds(oField){ return val.validateFunds(oField); } function validateEduDateInPresentOrPast(oFromYear, oFromMonth){ return val.validateEduDateInPresentOrPast(oFromYear, oFromMonth); } function validateEduCrossRef(oFromYYYY, oFromMM, oToYYYY, oToMM){ return val.validateEduCrossRef(oFromYYYY, oFromMM, oToYYYY, oToMM); } function validateIntlNum(oField){ return val.validateIntlNum(oField); } function validatePOBText(oField){ return val.validatePOBText(oField); } function validateDuplicateName(oGivenName, oFamilyName, oAliasName, oAliasFamilyName){ return val.validateDuplicateName(oGivenName, oFamilyName, oAliasName, oAliasFamilyName); } /*************************************************************************************************************************************************************************************** *utilDates script object contains all functions related to dates and date comparisons ****************************************************************************************************************************************************************************************/ /********************************************************************************* *Parameter: DateA as string, operator string, DateB as string *Compares the two dates based on the operator *Returns String Array: result[0] = bool, result[1] = error message *Chan Chac march 11, 2011 **********************************************************************************/ function XRefValidate(sDateA, sOperator, sDateB){ var sResult = new Array(); sResult[0] = true; sResult[1] = "default"; var dateA, dateB; if(sDateA != "" && sDateB != "" ){ //check if Date is Valid if (sResult[0]){ sResult = isValidDate(sDateB); } //compare both dates if (sResult[0]){ //convert both dates to date structures dateA = new MyDate(sDateA.substr(0,4), sDateA.substr(5,2), sDateA.substr(8,2) ); dateB = new MyDate(sDateB.substr(0,4), sDateB.substr(5,2), sDateB.substr(8,2) ); sResult = dateCompareWithStars(dateA, sOperator, dateB); } } return sResult; } function XRefValidateNID(sDateA, sOperator, sDateB){ var sResult = new Array(); sResult[0] = true; sResult[1] = "default"; var dateA, dateB; //check if Date is Valid if (sResult[0]){ sResult = isValidDateNID(sDateA); } //compare both dates if (sResult[0]){ //convert both dates to date structures dateA = new MyDate(sDateA.substr(0,4), sDateA.substr(5,2), sDateA.substr(8,2) ); dateB = new MyDate(sDateB.substr(0,4), sDateB.substr(5,2), sDateB.substr(8,2) ); sResult = dateCompareWithStars(dateA, sOperator, dateB); } return sResult; } /********************************************************************************* *Parameter: a date in String format *Returns true if the string is a valid date in the calendar, and has the proper *characters. *Returns String Array: result[0] = bool, result[1] = error message *Chan Chac march 11, 2011 **********************************************************************************/ function isValidDate(sDate){ var sResult = new Array(); sResult[0] = true; sResult[1] = "default"; var sDateArray; sResult = util.validateFormat("calendarDate", sDate); //check date is valid format if(sResult[0] == false) { sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Date") +"\n\n"+ sResult[1]; } //Check if date exist if (sResult[0]){ sDateArray = reformatHelper(sDate); if (!utilDates.isDateReal(sDateArray[0], sDateArray[1], sDateArray[2])){ sResult[0] = false; sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Date_Invalid"); } } return sResult; } function isValidDateNID(sDate){ var sResult = new Array(); sResult[0] = true; sResult[1] = "default"; var sDateArray; sResult = util.validateFormat("calendarDate", sDate); //check date is valid format if(sResult[0] == false) { sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Date") +"\n\n"+ sResult[1]; } //Check if date exist if (sResult[0]){ sDateArray = reformatHelper(sDate); if (!utilDates.isDateRealNID(sDateArray[0], sDateArray[1], sDateArray[2])){ sResult[0] = false; if( sDateArray[0] < 1900){ sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Date_Invalid"); }else{ sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Date_Invalid2"); } } } return sResult; } /********************************************************************************* *Breaks a date string into an array *Returns an Array array[0] = sYYYY, array[1] = sMM, array[2] = sDD *Parameter: a date string ie 2011-01-01 *Chan Chac march 11, 2011 **********************************************************************************/ function reformatHelper(sDate){ var sDateArray = new Array(); if(sDate.length == 10){ //parse the date sDateArray[0] = sDate.substr(0,4); sDateArray[1] = sDate.substr(5,2); sDateArray[2] = sDate.substr(8,2); } else if (sDate.length == 8){ //parse the date sDateArray[0] = sDate.substr(0,4); sDateArray[1] = sDate.substr(4,2); sDateArray[2] = sDate.substr(6,2); } return sDateArray; } /********************************************************************************* * Returns a date structure object given a year, month, day string * Chan Chac may 12 , 2011 **********************************************************************************/ function MyDate(sYear, sMonth, sDay){ this.year = sYear; this.month = sMonth; this.day = sDay; this.date = sYear + "-" + sMonth + "-" + sDay; return this; } /********************************************************************************* *a function check if a string is in the format *, **, ***, or **** *Returns boolean *Parameter: string **********************************************************************************/ function isStars(sNum){ return(sNum == "*" || sNum == "**" || sNum == "****" ||sNum == "*****" ); } /********************************************************************************* *Returns today date as a string, dash separated format *Returns String ie. 2007-12-31 **********************************************************************************/ function getToday(){ var today = new Date(); var MM = today.getMonth() + 1;//Jan is 0 var YYYY = today.getYear() + 1900; var DD = today.getDate(); (DD <10) ? (DD ='0'+ DD) : DD; (MM<10) ? (MM ='0'+ MM) : MM ; var result = YYYY + "-" + MM + "-" + DD ; return result; } /********************************************************************************* *a function to handle stars (wild cards) when comparing two dates *Returns an Array array[0] = true/false, array[1] = error message *Parameter: string d1Yr, d1MM, d1DD, operator, d2Yr, d2MM, d2DD **********************************************************************************/ function dateCompareWithStars(oDateA, operator, oDateB){ //Define an array to return boolean and an error message var sResult = new Array(); sResult[0] = false; sResult[1] = null; switch (operator) { case "=": case "==": case ">": case ">=": case "<": case "<=": //////////////////////////////////Handle Stars Here//////////////////////////////// if ((isStars(oDateA.year) || isStars(oDateB.year)) ) sResult[0]= true; else if (((oDateA.year == oDateB.year) && (isStars(oDateA.month) || isStars(oDateB.month)))) sResult[0]= true; else if ((oDateA.year == oDateB.year) && (oDateA.month == oDateB.month) && ((isStars(oDateA.day) || isStars(oDateB.day)))) sResult[0]= true;   if(!sResult[0]){ if (isStars(oDateA.month) || isStars(oDateB.month)){ oDateA.month = "0"; oDateB.month = "0"; sResult = dateCompare(oDateA, operator, oDateB); } if (isStars(oDateA.day) || isStars(oDateB.day)){ oDateA.day= "0"; oDateB.day= "0"; sResult = dateCompare(oDateA, operator, oDateB); } else{ //no stars found sResult = dateCompare(oDateA, operator, oDateB); } } break; default: break; }//end switch return sResult; } /********************************************************************************* *Compares two date strings based on the operator *Returns an Array array[0] = true/false, array[1] = error message *Parameter: d1Yr, d1MM, d1DD, operator, d2Yr, d2MM, d2DD *Chan Chac march 23, 2011 **********************************************************************************/ function dateCompare(oDateA, operator, oDateB){ //Define an array to return boolean and an error message var sResult = new Array(); sResult[0] = true; sResult[1] = null; var NIDExpiryDate = xfa.resolveNode("form1.Page2.natID.natIDdocs.ExpiryDate"); var NIDIssueDate = xfa.resolveNode("form1.Page2.natID.natIDdocs.IssueDate.IssueDate"); switch(operator) { case ">": case ">=": if (oDateA.year < oDateB.year){ sResult[0] = false; sResult[1] = "To date must be greater than from date"; } else { if (oDateA.year == oDateB.year ){ if (oDateA.month < oDateB.month){ sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Month_Current_Error"); sResult[0] = false; } else if (oDateA.month == oDateB.month) { //when operator == > if (operator == ">" && oDateA.day <= oDateB.day){ sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Day_Current_Error"); sResult[0] = false; } //when operator is >= if (operator == ">=" && oDateA.day < oDateB.day){ sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Day_Current_Error"); sResult[0] = false; } } } } break; case "<": case "<=": if (oDateA.year > oDateB.year){ sResult[0] = false; sResult[1] = "Year is greater error"; } else { if (oDateA.year == oDateB.year ){ if (oDateA.month > oDateB.month){ sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Month_Current_Error"); sResult[0] = false; } else if (oDateA.month == oDateB.month) { //when operator is < if (operator == "<" && oDateA.day >= oDateB.day){ sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Day_Current_Error"); sResult[0] = false; } //when operator is < if (operator == "<=" && oDateA.day > oDateB.day){ sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Day_Current_Error"); sResult[0] = false; } } } } break; case "==": case "=": if (oDateA.year != oDateB.year || oDateA.month != oDateB.month|| oDateA.day != oDateB.day){ sResult[1] = "the two dates are not equal"; sResult[0] = false; } break; default: } //handle message I will revisit this section for cleanup if (!sResult[0]){ switch(operator) { case ">": if ((!NIDExpiryDate.isNull) && (!NIDIssueDate.isNull)) { sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Expiry date must be greater than Issue Date") ; } else{ sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("To date must be greater than From Date") ; } break; case ">=": sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("To date must be greater than or equal to From Date"); break; case "<": if ((!NIDExpiryDate.isNull) && (!NIDIssueDate.isNull)) { sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Issue date must be less than Expiry Date") ; } else{ sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("To date must be less than From Date"); } break; case "<=": sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("To date must be less than or equal to From Date"); break; case "==": sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("To date must be equal to From Date"); break; } } //I will revisit this section for cleanup. //Handle message if date is compared against today if (!sResult[0] && (oDateB.date == getToday()) ){ switch(operator) { case ">": sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Date must be Greater than Today's date"); break; case ">=": sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Date must be Greater than or equal to Today's Date"); break; case "<": sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Date must be less than Today Date"); break; case "<=": sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Date must be less than or equal to Today's Date"); break; case "==": sResult[1] = messages.getMessage("Date must be equal to Today's Date"); break; } } return sResult; } /************************************************************************************ * Validates date is real taking into consideration leap year * * Input Params * year = Year (actual data) * month = 1-12 * day = 1-31 * strYrXpath, strMMXPath, strDDXPath = string of object path. * * Output: boolean * true = valid date/false = invalid date. * * CPARK 9/23/09 edited by Chan Chac ************************************************************************************/ function isDateReal(year, month, day) { try { var result = true; if (util.validateField("Year", year)) // checks less than today + > 1900 or '*' { if (util.validateField("Month", month)) { if (util.validateField("Day", day)) { result = true; // check if num and convert so i dont have to deal with leading zeros. if (util.isNumeric(year)) var numYr = (+year); if (util.isNumeric(month)) var numMonth = (+month); if (util.isNumeric(day)) var numDay = (+day); // 3 types of months, ends in 29 (leap yr), 30 or 31. switch(numMonth) { case 1: // Jan case 3: // March case 5: // May case 7: // July case 8: // Aug case 10:// Oct case 12:// Dec if (numDay > 31) { //MarkField(false, strDDXPath); result = false; } else result = true; break; case 2: // Feb if (numDay < 30) { if (numDay == 29 && util.isNumeric(year)) //if 29 and Yr != * { result = isLeapYear(numYr); } else //less than 29 { //MarkField(true, strDDXPath); result = true; } } else { //MarkField(false, strDDXPath); result = false; } break; case 4: // April case 6: // June case 9: // Sept case 11:// Nov if (numDay > 30) result = false; break; default: // "*****" result = true; break; } } else result = false; } else result = false; } // end yr else result = false; return result; } catch(e) { console.show(); console.println(e.toString()); throw e } } function isDateRealNID(year, month, day) { try { var result = true; if (util.validateFieldNID("Year", year)) // checks less than today + > 1900 or '*' { if (util.validateFieldNID("Month", month)) { if (util.validateFieldNID("Day", day)) { result = true; // check if num and convert so i dont have to deal with leading zeros. if (util.isNumeric(year)) var numYr = (+year); if (util.isNumeric(month)) var numMonth = (+month); if (util.isNumeric(day)) var numDay = (+day); // 3 types of months, ends in 29 (leap yr), 30 or 31. switch(numMonth) { case 1: // Jan case 3: // March case 5: // May case 7: // July case 8: // Aug case 10:// Oct case 12:// Dec if (numDay > 31) { //MarkField(false, strDDXPath); result = false; } else result = true; break; case 2: // Feb if (numDay < 30) { if (numDay == 29 && util.isNumeric(year)) //if 29 and Yr != * { result = isLeapYear(numYr); } else //less than 29 { //MarkField(true, strDDXPath); result = true; } } else { //MarkField(false, strDDXPath); result = false; } break; case 4: // April case 6: // June case 9: // Sept case 11:// Nov if (numDay > 30) result = false; break; default: // "*****" result = true; break; } } else result = false; } else result = false; } // end yr else result = false; return result; } catch(e) { console.show(); console.println(e.toString()); throw e } } /************************************************************************************ * Validates date of Birth as a whole is real taking into consideration leap year * * Input Params * year = Year (actual data) * month = 1-12 * day = 1-31 * * Output: String * true = valid date/false = invalid date. * * CPARK 9/23/09 edited by Chan Chac ************************************************************************************/ function validateDateOfBirth(year, month, day) { try { var result = new Array(); result[0] = true; result[1] = ""; //this can be done with regular expression later. //check for special case YYYY/**/DD, this is not allowed. Chan Chac if (!util.hasAlphas(year) && (month == "**" || month == "*") && !util.hasAlphas(day)) { result[1] = messages.getMessage("DOB_Day_Must_Be_Star_Or_Month_Must_Be_Numeric"); result[0] = false } //check for special case Stars/Stars/DD, this is not allowed. Chan Chac if ((year == "*" || year == "**" || year == "***" || year == "****") && (month == "**" || month == "*") && !util.hasAlphas(day)) { result[1] = messages.getMessage("DOB_Day_Must_Be_Star"); result[0]= false; } //check for special case Stars/MM/DD, this is not allowed. Chan Chac if ((year == "*" || year == "**" || year == "***" || year == "****") && (!util.hasAlphas(month)) && !util.hasAlphas(day)) { result[1] = messages.getMessage("DOB_Year_Must_Be_Numeric"); result[0] = false; } //check for special case Stars/MM/Stars, this is not allowed. Chan Chac if ((year == "*" || year == "**" || year == "***" || year == "****") && (!util.hasAlphas(month)) && (day == "*" || day == "**")) { result[1]= messages.getMessage("DOB_Month_Must_Be_Star"); result[0] = false; } return result; } catch(e) { console.show(); console.println(e.toString()); throw e } }//end function /********************************************************************************* *Validates the day of range of a date and returns the proper error message *Returns an Array array[0] = true/false, array[1] = error message *Parameter: d1Yr, d1MM, d1DD *Chan Chac march 23, 2011 *CPARK validate > 0 PR# GCMS100067020 *CPARK 12/19/11 74977 **********************************************************************************/ function validateDayRange(numYr, numMonth, numDay){ var result = new Array() result[0] = true; result[1] = ""; if (isStars(numYr) || numYr == "" || numYr == null) numYr = "2011"; //use a non leap-year year. if(isStars(numMonth) || numMonth == "" || numMonth == null) numMonth = "1"; // 3 types of months, ends in 29 (leap yr), 30 or 31. switch(numMonth) { case "1": // Jan case "3": // March case "5": // May case "7": // July case "8": // Aug case "10": // Oct case "12": // Dec if (numDay > 31 || numDay < 1) // validate > 0 PR# GCMS100067020 { result[0] = false; result[1]= messages.getMessage("DOB_Day"); } break; case "2": // Feb if (isLeapYear(numYr)){ //days must be between 1-29 if ((numDay < 30 && util.isNumeric(numDay) && numDay > 0) || isStars(numDay)){ // validate > 0 PR# GCMS100067020 //PR#74977 isStars } else{ result[0] = false; result[1] = messages.getMessage("Day_Must_Be_Less_Than_29"); } } else{ //days must be between 1-28 if ((numDay < 29 && util.isNumeric(numDay) && numDay > 0) || isStars(numDay)){ // validate > 0 PR# GCMS100067020 //PR#74977 isStars } else{ result[0] = false; result[1] = messages.getMessage("Day_Must_Be_Less_Than_28"); } } break; case "4": // April case "6": // June case "9": // Sept case "11":// Nov if (numDay > 30 || numDay < 1){ // validate > 0 PR# GCMS100067020 result[0] = false; result[1] = messages.getMessage("30_Day"); } break; default: // "*****" break; } return result; } /* * Validates whether LeapYear is a LeapYear. ie. Feb 29 exists. * Input Param: * leapYear = field value * * OUput Param: boolean * true - leap yr/false - regular yr. * * CPARK 9/23/09 ************************************************************************************/ function isLeapYear(leapYear) { try { var dteIsLeap = new Date(leapYear,1,29); //Feb 29, leapYear if (dteIsLeap.getDate() == 29) return true; else return false; // getDate returns 1 } catch(e) { console.show(); console.println(e.toString()); throw e } finally { dteIsLeap = null; } } ////////////////////////Validate UCI field////////////////////////////////////// function validateUCI(oUCI){ var result = new Array(); result[0] = true; if (oUCI.isNull) { color.markCorrect(oUCI); } else{ //trim leading spaces, trailing spaces, and replace carriage returns with space oUCI.rawValue = util.trim(oUCI.rawValue); //oUCI.rawValue = util.trim(oUCI.rawValue); result = util.validateFormat("Digits", oUCI.rawValue); if (!result[0]){ //incorrect value color.markIncorrect(oUCI); if(globShowErrorMessageFlag.value == "true"){ xfa.host.messageBox(oUCI.formatMessage +"\n"+ messages.getMessage("Format_Entered")+ "\n" + oUCI.validationMessage + "\n\n" + messages.getMessage("UCI") + "\n\n" + result[1]); } validateForm.setGlobFirstField(oUCI); } else color.markCorrect(oUCI); } return result; } ////////////////////////Validate DropDown////////////////////////////////////// function validateDropDown(oField){ var result = new Array(); result[0] = true; if (oField.mandatory == "error"){ if(!oField.isNull && oField.rawValue != 'null'){ color.markCorrect(oField); } else { color.markRequired(oField); result[0] = false; } } return result; } ////////////////////////Validate Name////////////////////////////////////// function validateName(oField){ var result = new Array(); result[0] = true; if (oField.isNull) { color.markCorrect(oField); } else { //trim leading spaces, trailing spaces, and replace carriage returns with space oField.rawValue = util.trim(oField.rawValue); //check for leading space result = util.checkLeadingSpace(oField.rawValue); if(result[0]) result = util.validateFormat("Name", oField.rawValue); if(!result[0]){ //incorrect if(globShowErrorMessageFlag.value == "true"){ xfa.host.messageBox(oField.formatMessage +"\n"+ messages.getMessage("Format_Entered")+ "\n" + oField.validationMessage + "\n\n" + messages.getMessage("Family Name") + "\n\n" + result[1], "Validation Error"); } color.markIncorrect(oField); validateForm.setGlobFirstField(oField); } else color.markCorrect(oField); } return result; } //////////////////Validates Place of Birth City format//////////////////////////// function validateOpenText(oField){ var result = new Array(); result[0] = true; if (oField.isNull) { color.markCorrect(oField); } else { //trim leading spaces, trailing spaces, and replace carriage returns with space oField.rawValue = util.trim(oField.rawValue); //oField.rawValue = util.trim(oField.rawValue); result = util.validateFormat("Open Text", oField.rawValue) if (!result[0]){ //incorrect if(globShowErrorMessageFlag.value == "true"){ xfa.host.messageBox(oField.formatMessage +"\n"+ messages.getMessage("Format_Entered")+ "\n" + oField.validationMessage + "\n\n" + messages.getMessage("Open Text") + "\n\n" + result[1], "Validation Error"); } color.markIncorrect(oField); validateForm.setGlobFirstField(oField); } else color.markCorrect(oField); } return result; } //Compares two DateA vs DateB based on the operator //returns an array where array[0] = boolean, array[1] is error message //paramters: Date Field, operator string, Date String function validateDate(oDateA, sOperator, sDateB){ var result = new Array(); var dateA, dateB; result[0] = true; if (oDateA.isNull) { color.markCorrect(oDateA); } else { //perform single date validation result = utilDates.isValidDate(oDateA.rawValue); //perform cross reference validation of two dates if (result[0]){ result = utilDates.XRefValidate(oDateA.rawValue, sOperator, sDateB); } if(!result[0]){ color.markIncorrect(oDateA); if(globShowErrorMessageFlag.value == "true"){ xfa.host.messageBox(oDateA.formatMessage +"\n"+ messages.getMessage("Format_Entered")+ "\n" + oDateA.validationMessage + "\n\n" + result[1], messages.getMessage("Validation Error")); } validateForm.setGlobFirstField(oDateA); } else{ color.markCorrect(oDateA); } } return result; } function validateDateNID(oDateA, sOperator, sDateB){ var result = new Array(); var dateA, dateB; result[0] = true; if (oDateA.isNull ) { color.markCorrect(oDateA); } else { //perform single date validation result = utilDates.isValidDateNID(oDateA.rawValue); //perform cross reference validation of two dates if (result[0]){ if(!oDateA.isNull && !sDateB.isNull){ result = utilDates.XRefValidateNID(oDateA.rawValue, sOperator, sDateB.rawValue); "8*" or "*7" for DD * findInvalidCharacters doesnt catch this case since it compares each char by itself */ if (!result[0] && result[1] == "") { switch(oMM.rawValue) { case "1": //1-31 case "3": // March case "5": // May case "7": // July case "8": // Aug case "10": // Oct case "12": // Dec result[1] = messages.getMessage("DOB_Day") break; case "2": //1-28/29 if (utilDates.isLeapYear(oYYYY.rawValue)) result[1] = messages.getMessage("Day_Must_Be_Less_Than_29") else result[1] = messages.getMessage("Day_Must_Be_Less_Than_28") break; default: //1-30 result[1] = messages.getMessage("30_Day") break; } //end switch } //validate the Range is correct if (result[0]) result = utilDates.validateDayRange(oYYYY.rawValue, oMM.rawValue, oDD.rawValue); if(!result[0]){ color.markIncorrect(oDD); if(globShowErrorMessageFlag.value == "true"){ xfa.host.messageBox(oDD.formatMessage +"\n"+ messages.getMessage("Format_Entered")+ "\n" + oDD.validationMessage + "\n\n" + result[1] , "Validation Error"); } validateForm.setGlobFirstField(oDD); oAge.rawValue = null; //oAge.execEvent("exit"); } else{ color.markCorrect(oDD); if(!oYYYY.isNull && !oMM.isNull && !oDD.isNull && !color.isMarkedRedAlready(oYYYY) && !color.isMarkedRedAlready(oMM) && !color.isMarkedRedAlready(oDD)) result = checkDOBAsWhole(oYYYY, oMM, oDD, oAge); } } } return result; } /********************************************************************************* * Validates DOB YEAR, DOB MONTH, and DOB DAY as a whole * Chan Chac march 11, 2011 **********************************************************************************/ function checkDOBAsWhole(oYYYY, oMM, oDD, oAge){ var result = new Array(); result[0] = true; result[1] = ""; var sAge = ""; //check entire date as a whole (ie. + month; if (day.length == 1) day = "0" + day; myDate = year +"-"+month+"-"+ day; result = utilDates.XRefValidate(myDate, "<=", utilDates.getToday()); if (result[0]) result = color.markCorrect(oAliasFamilyName); } return result; } var WHITE = "255,255,255"; var RED = "232, 6, 6"; //e80606 (Was formerly 255,0,0) var INVALID_FILL_COLOR = "255, 128, 128"; //was formerly 255, 128,128 var INVALID_BORDER_COLOR = "231, 6, 6"; //off-red //"0,255,0"; //green var DISABLED_COLOR = "233, 233, 233"; //#E9E9E9 // Returns the Acrobat Highlight Color in XFA's RGB format. function GetAcrobatHLColor() { var aAcroColor = app.runtimeHighlightColor; // returns an array: ["RGB",R,G,B] (RGB value) or ["G",G] (gray value) // In the color array, the red, green and blue components are represented by values between 0 (no color) and 1 (full color). // We want the color represented as values between 0 (no color) and 255 (full color). if (aAcroColor[0] == "RGB") { // RGB value -- straight-forward return Math.round(aAcroColor[1] * 255) + "," + Math.round(aAcroColor[2] * 255) + "," + Math.round(aAcroColor[3] * 255); } else if (aAcroColor[0] == "G") { // Gray value -- apply same intensity to all components return Math.round(aAcroColor[1] * 255) + "," + Math.round(aAcroColor[1] * 255) + "," + Math.round(aAcroColor[1] * 255); } else { // don't know how to translate color into RGB return ""; } } // Set the highlight color. function SetColor(objectType, sColor) { objectType.border.fill.color.value = sColor; } // Set the the field to disabled color. function INVALID_BORDER_COLOR)) } // Returns true if the field is mandatory and isn't filled. function IsEmpty(oField) { var sClassName = oField.className; // Only color nodes that are <field>s or <exclGroup>s // Ignore everything else if ((sClassName != "field") && (sClassName != "exclGroup")) { return false; } // If the node is a <field> that is a button or a barcode, then don't // do any color processing if (sClassName == "field") { var sUIClassName = oField.ui.oneOfChild.className; if ((sUIClassName == "barcode") || (sUIClassName == "button")) { return false; } } /*if(isMarkedRedAlready(oField)){ return true; //dont process if its already RED. }*/ if (oField.mandatory == "error" && (oField.isNull)){ return true; // mandatory field } //choice list box with dynamic LOV's if (oField.mandatory == "error" && oField.className == "field" && oField.ui.oneOfChild.className == "choiceList" && oField.rawValue == "null"){ return true; // mandatory field } // If the field's parent is an exclusion group, the exclusion group's mandatory setting applies to all fields it contains. if (oField.parent.className == "exclGroup" && oField.parent.mandatory == "error" && oField.parent.rawValue.length == 0){ return true; // parent is a mandatory exclusion group (e.g. radio button list) } if (oField.ui.oneOfChild.className == "checkButton" && oField.validate.nullTest == "error") { // Check boxes always have a default value (so it won't be null neither will it be an empty string). // If a check box is mandatory, we'll assume that means it's value should either be "on" or "neutral" but not "off". // (Note that while check boxes can't be made mandatory via Designer's UI, they can be via script: See the Initialize script of the // CheckBox3 field.) // By definition (in the XFA 2.2 spec), the "off" value is the second value in the first <items> child node. var oItemsList = oField.resolveNodes("#items"); // note the "#" prefix to access the <items> child nodes explicitely if (oItemsList.length > 0) { var oValueList = oItemsList.item(0).nodes; if (oValueList.length > 1) { var sOffValue = oValueList.item(1).value; if (oField.rawValue == sOffValue) return true; // found a mandatory check box whose value is still set to "off" } } } return false; } ///////////////////////////////////////// // Parameters: a node object // Action: checks the class type. // Helper function to make life easier // Returns: the class Type as a string //////////////////////////////////////// function GetType(oNode) { var type; if (oNode.className == "field") { switch (oNode.ui.oneOfChild.className) { case oUITypeNode = oNode.ui.resolveNode("#choiceList"); if (oUITypeNode.open == "userControl") { // drop down list / combo box type = "#choiceList"; } else { // otherwise, it's a list box type = "#choiceList"; } break; } case "dateTimeEdit": type = "#dateTimeEdit"; break; case "imageEdit": "#imageEdit" break; case "numericEdit": type = "#numericEdit"; break; case "passwordEdit": type = "#passwordEdit"; break; case "textEdit": type = "#textEdit"; break; case "barcode": type = "#barcode"; break; default: // unknown field type break; } } return type; } ///////////////////////////////////////// // Parameters: a node object // Action: Paints the obeject white // Returns: nothing //////////////////////////////////////// function markCorrect(oNode){ } if(oNode.access == "readOnly") color.SetColorDisabled(oNode); } ///////////////////////////////////////// // Parameters: a node object // Action: Paints the object Red // Returns: nothing ///////////////////////////////////////// // Parameters: a node object // Action: Paints the object Red just the border only // Returns: nothing //////////////////////////////////////// function markRequired(oNode){ //add an asterisk to the label sCaption = util.getCaption(oNode); if (sCaption != null && sCaption != ""){ if(sCaption.charAt(0) != "*"){ util.setCaption(oNode, "*" + sCaption); } } oNode.mandatory = "error"; //handle checkboxes if (oNode.className == "field" && oNode.ui.oneOfChild.className == "checkButton" && oNode.parent.className == "exclGroup"){ oNode.parent.mandatory = validateForm.updateDirtyFlag(oNode); } } ///////////////////////////////////////// // Parameters: a node object // Action: Paints the object back to unrequired state // Returns: nothing (!isMarkedRedAlready(oNode)) markCorrect(oNode); } ///////////////////////////////////////// // Parameters: a node object // Action: changes a field to accepts multiline text // Returns: nothing //////////////////////////////////////// function makeMultiLine(oNode){ if (oNode.ui.oneOfChild.className == "textEdit"){ oNode.resolveNode("ui.#textEdit").vScrollPolicy = "off"; oNode.resolveNode("ui.#textEdit").hScrollPolicy = "off"; oNode.resolveNode("ui.#textEdit").multiLine = "1"; } } ///////////////////////////////////////// // Parameters: a node object // Action: changes a field to accepts single line text // Returns: nothing //////////////////////////////////////// function makeSingleLine(oNode){ if (oNode.ui.oneOfChild.className == "textEdit"){ oNode.resolveNode("ui.#textEdit").vScrollPolicy = "off"; oNode.resolveNode("ui.#textEdit").hScrollPolicy = "auto"; oNode.resolveNode("ui.#textEdit").multiLine = "0"; } } //This function is taken from Designer 9 and has been customized //Changes the appearance of the field by coloring it //This function is taken from Designer 9 and has been customized //Changes the appearance of the field by coloring it function DoColorFields(oInvalidNode, validFlag, fillFlag) { // If this form is running on a client other than Acrobat // (like on the server) then don't run this script if (xfa.host.name != "Acrobat") { return; } var sClassName = oInvalidNode.className; // Only color nodes that are <field>s or <exclGroup>s // Ignore everything else if ((sClassName != "field") && (sClassName != "exclGroup")) { return; } // If the node is a <field> that is a button or a barcode, then don't // do any color processing if (sClassName == "field") { var sUIClassName = oInvalidNode.ui.oneOfChild.className; if ((sUIClassName == "barcode") || (sUIClassName == "button")) { return; } } if (validFlag) { // Validation Succeeded // Revert the appearance to its original state var oBorder = sClassName == "field" ? oInvalidNode.ui.oneOfChild.border : oInvalidNode.border; oBorder.parent.nodes.remove(oBorder); // Remove the tool tip if it wasn?t originally specified /*var sSOM = oInvalidNode.somExpression; sSOM = sSOM.replace("xfa[0].form[0]", "xfa[0].template[0]"); var oOriginalNode = xfa.resolveNode(sSOM); var oOriginalAssist = oOriginalNode.assist; if (!oOriginalAssist.isPropertySpecified("toolTip")) { var oToolTip = oInvalidNode.assist.toolTip; oInvalidNode.assist.nodes.remove(oToolTip); }*/ } else { // Validation Failed // Show the invalid appearance var oFailedBorder = sClassName == "field" ? oInvalidNode.ui.oneOfChild.border : oInvalidNode.border; // Border color // Show a solid border with square corners var sBorderColor = RED; //"255, 0, 0"; if (fillFlag){ //show an incorrect border color. //note: the failed border color is similar but not the same color to a required field. //a failed border marks things as incorrect, not to be mistaken with required. sBorderColor = INVALID_BORDER_COLOR; } oFailedBorder.presence = "visible"; for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) { var oEdge = oFailedBorder.getElement("edge", i); oEdge.presence = "visible"; oEdge.color.value = sBorderColor; oEdge.thickness = "2pt"; oEdge.stroke = "solid"; var oCorner = oFailedBorder.getElement("corner", i); oCorner.presence = "visible"; oCorner.color.value = sBorderColor; oCorner.thickness = "2pt"; oCorner.stroke = "solid"; oCorner.join = "square"; oCorner.inverted = "0"; oCorner.radius = "0mm"; } /* //We no longer Fill fields with Red due to accessibility. if (fillFlag){ // Background color // Show a solid fill color oFailedBorder.fill.color.value = INVALID_FILL_COLOR; } */ // The presence of the border must be visible to show the fill. // Hide the edges when the invalid appearance doesn't include // changing the border color if (oFailedBorder.presence != "visible") { oFailedBorder.presence = "visible"; oFailedBorder.edge.presence = "invisible"; } // Replace the current fill type with a solid fill if (oFailedBorder.fill.oneOfChild.className != "solid") { var oFailedFillType = oFailedBorder.fill.oneOfChild; oFailedBorder.fill.nodes.remove(oFailedFillType); var oSolid = xfa.form.createNode("solid", ""); oFailedBorder.fill.nodes.append(oSolid); } } } //Same function as above but it takes in 4 paramters function DoColorFieldsWithColors(oInvalidNode, validFlag, sInvalidBackgroundColor, sInvalidBorderColor) { // If this form is running on a client other than Acrobat // (like on the server) then don't run this script if (xfa.host.name != "Acrobat") { return; } var sClassName = oInvalidNode.className; // Only color nodes that are <field>s or <exclGroup>s // Ignore everything else if ((sClassName != "field") && (sClassName != "exclGroup")) { return; } // If the node is a <field> that is a button or a barcode, then don't // do any color processing if (sClassName == "field") { var sUIClassName = oInvalidNode.ui.oneOfChild.className; if ((sUIClassName == "barcode") || (sUIClassName == "button")) { return; } } if (validFlag) { // Validation Succeeded // Revert the appearance to its original state var oBorder = sClassName == "field" ? oInvalidNode.ui.oneOfChild.border : oInvalidNode.border; oBorder.parent.nodes.remove(oBorder); // Remove the tool tip if it wasn?t originally specified var sSOM = oInvalidNode.somExpression; sSOM = sSOM.replace("xfa[0].form[0]", "xfa[0].template[0]"); var oOriginalNode = xfa.resolveNode(sSOM); var oOriginalAssist = oOriginalNode.assist; if (!oOriginalAssist.isPropertySpecified("toolTip")) { var oToolTip = oInvalidNode.assist.toolTip; oInvalidNode.assist.nodes.remove(oToolTip); } } else { // Validation Failed // Show the invalid appearance var oFailedBorder = sClassName == "field" ? oInvalidNode.ui.oneOfChild.border : oInvalidNode.border; // Border color // Show a solid border with square corners var sBorderColor = sInvalidBorderColor; oFailedBorder.presence = "visible"; for (var i = 0; i < 4; i++) { var oEdge = oFailedBorder.getElement("edge", i); oEdge.presence = "visible"; oEdge.color.value = sBorderColor; oEdge.thickness = "1pt"; oEdge.stroke = "solid"; var oCorner = oFailedBorder.getElement("corner", i); oCorner.presence = "visible"; oCorner.color.value = sBorderColor; oCorner.thickness = "1pt"; oCorner.stroke = "solid"; oCorner.join = "square"; oCorner.inverted = "0"; oCorner.radius = "0mm"; } // Background color // Show a solid fill color oFailedBorder.fill.color.value = sInvalidBackgroundColor; // The presence of the border must be visible to show the fill. // Hide the edges when the invalid appearance doesn't include // changing the border color if (oFailedBorder.presence != "visible") { oFailedBorder.presence = "visible"; oFailedBorder.edge.presence = "invisible"; } // Replace the current fill type with a solid fill if (oFailedBorder.fill.oneOfChild.className != "solid") { var oFailedFillType = oFailedBorder.fill.oneOfChild; oFailedBorder.fill.nodes.remove(oFailedFillType); var oSolid = xfa.form.createNode("solid", ""); oFailedBorder.fill.nodes.append(oSolid); } } } /*************************************************************************************************************************************************************************************** *util script object contains validation functions and useful functions ****************************************************************************************************************************************************************************************/ /* * Enable/disable and clears Details field * for Statutory Q * * Input: strXPath = xfa path * bEnable = true/false * Output: enables details field. * * CPARK 11/18/09 *************************************************************/ function enableField(strXPath, bEnable) { try { var oField = xfa.resolveNode(strXPath); if (bEnable) //enable { oField.access = "open"; color.SetColorEnabled(oField); } else { oField.access = "readOnly"; color.SetColorDisabled(oField); oField.rawValue = null; } } catch(e) { console.show(); console.println(e.toString()); throw e; } finally { oField = null; } } //checks if a string has alpha characters function hasAlphas(someString) { var re = /^[0-9]*$/; return (!re.test(someString)); } //UCI validation function validateUCI(someString) { //var re = /^\d{8}$|^T\d{8,12}$/; var re = /^[0-9]/ return (re.test(someString)); } //UCI validation function validatePhone(someString) { var re = /^(\+?1?\s*[-\/\.]?)?(\((\d+)\)|(\d+))\s*[-\/\.]?\s*(\d+)\s*[-\/\.]?\s*(\d+)\s*(\s*(\d+))*$/; return (re.test(someString)); } /* AppNo# validation * SREQ 8589 - App# starts with 'B' followed by 9 digits with last digit being a Mod 7 check. * via RegExp * * Input - strAppNum - app num * Ouput - boolean * * CPARK 11/18/09 * CPARK 12/1/09 updated script based on TBUI calc. **************************************************************/ function validateAppNum(strAppNum) { try { var re = /^B\d{9}$/; //"B" + 9 chars if (re.test(strAppNum)) { //validate last digit is mod 7 check digit var strVal = strAppNum.substring(1, strAppNum.length - 1); var intChkDigit = strAppNum.substring(strAppNum.length - 1); if (strVal % 7 == intChkDigit) return true; else return false; } else return false; } catch(e) { console.show(); console.println(e.toString()); throw e } } //checks if a t is a canadian postal code (e.g., "T2X 1V4" or "T2X1V4") function isCanadianPostalCode(t) { t = trimAll(t); var regExpr = /^[A-Z]\d[A-Z] \d[A-Z]\d$/ var regExpr2 = /^[A-Z]\d[A-Z]\d[A-Z]\d$/ return (regExpr.test(t) || regExpr2.test(t) || isCanadianPostalCodeWithDash(t) ); } //checks if a t is a canadian postal code (e.g., "T2X 1V4" or "T2X1V4" or) function isCanadianPostalCodeWithDash(t) { t = trimAll(t); var regExpr = /^[A-Z]\d[A-Z]\-\d[A-Z]\d$/ var regExpr2 = /^[A-Z]\d[A-Z] \- \d[A-Z]\d$/ return (regExpr.test(t) || regExpr2.test(t) ); } //checks if a t is a canadian postal code (e.g., "T2X 1V4" or "T2X1V4") function isUSZipCode(t) { var regExpr = /^\d{5}(-\d{4})?$/ return regExpr.test(t); } /********************************************************************************************** * Calc height where 1 inch = 2.54cm * * Input Param: * imperialArray is an array of size 2. imperialArray[0] = ft, imperialArray[1] = inches * * Output PAram: Integer: Height in metric (cm) * * CPARK 9/10/09 ***********************************************************************************************/ function calcHeightCm(imperialArray) { try { var intCm = 0; var intFt = imperialArray[0]; var intInch = imperialArray[1]; intCm = (intFt * 30.48) + (intInch * 2.54); return intCm; } catch(e) { console.show(); console.println(e.toString()); throw e } } function setHeightCM(iheightFT, iheightIN, oheightCM){ var heightArr = new Array(); var intCmCalc; heightArr[0] = iheightFT; heightArr[1] = iheightIN; //calc cm and populate intCmCalc= util.calcHeightCm(heightArr); oheightCM.rawValue = intCmCalc; color.markCorrect(oheightCM); } /* * Validates field content (mainly date) * * Input Params: * fieldType = Year, Day, Month, DOBYear * fieldValue = field value * * Ouput Params: boolean * true - valid/false - invalid * * CPARK 9/9/09 ************************************************************************************/ function validateField(fieldType, fieldValue) { try { var result; (isStars(fieldValue)) { result = true; } else { result = false; } } break; case "DODYear": // var dteToday = new Date(); if (util.isNumeric(fieldValue)) //check is '*' is acceptable { var numValue = (+fieldValue); // cast to numeric from string if (numValue <= dteToday.getFullYear()) result = true; else result= false; } else // should be **** result= false; //DOD Year cannot be stars must be (isStars(fieldValue)) result = true; else{ result = false; } } break; case "Day": // if (util.isNumeric(fieldValue)) { var numValue = (+fieldValue); if (numValue <= 31 && numValue >= 1) result = true; else result= false; } else{ // should be**** if (isStars(fieldValue)) result= true; else result= false; } break; default: break } return result; } catch(e) { console.show(); console.println(e.toString()); throw e } } function validateFieldNID(fieldType, fieldValue) { try { var result; switch(fieldType) { case "Year": // if (util.isNumeric(fieldValue)) //check is '*' is acceptable { var numValue = (+fieldValue); // cast to numeric from string //if (numValue >= 1900 && numValue < 4733) //Upper and Lower bound of siebel date values if (numValue >= 1900 && numValue < (+fieldValue); // cast to numeric from string if (numValue >= 1900 && numValue <= dteToday.getFullYear()) result = true; else { result = false; } } else{ // should **** if (isStars(fieldValue)) { result = true; } else { result = false; } } break; case "DODYear": // var dteToday = new Date(); if (util.isNumeric(fieldValue)) //check is '*' is acceptable { var numValue = (+fieldValue); // cast to // if (util.isNumeric(fieldValue)) { var numValue = (+fieldValue); if (numValue <= 12 && numValue >= 1) result = true; else{ result = false; } } else{ // should be**** if (isStars(fieldValue)) result = true; else{ result = false; } } break; case "Day": // if (util.isNumeric(fieldValue)) { var numValue = (+fieldValue); if (numValue <= 31 && numValue >= 1) result = true; else result= false; } else{ // should be**** if (isStars(fieldValue)) result= true; else result= false; } break; default: break } return result; } catch(e) { console.show(); console.println(e.toString()); throw e } } /* * Validates field formats using Regular Expressions * Regular Expression parsed * * ^ - Matches beginning of input or line * $ - Matches end of input or line * [ - String must match whats inside * a-zA-Z = alpha upper+lower case, 0-9 = numeric, - = dashes, ' = apostrophes, " " = space, + = +, \- = -, () = () * ] * + - repeat til end of string * * NOTE: LiveCycle doesnt seem to like \s for spaces, or \- for dashes, etc * * Input Param: * fieldType = Name, IdDocument, Phone, Email * * Output Param: bolean * true - valid/false - invalid * CPARK 9/9/09 ************************************************************************************/ function validateFormat(fieldType, fieldValue) { try { //validate Format var result = new Array(); var r = new RegExp(); switch(fieldType) { case "IdDocument": //for passport number alphanum r.compile("^[ a-zA-Z0-9]+$"); result[0] = r.test(fieldValue); break; case "Funds": //for funds r.compile("^[0-9]+$"); result[0] = r.test(fieldValue); break; case "CountryCode": //for CountryCode r = /^1[0,2-9]{3}$|^[0,2-9]\d{0,2}$|^1[2-9]\d{2}$|^1$/ result[0] = r.test(fieldValue); if(!result[0]) r = /^[0-9]$/ break; case "PhoneNAN": //last four digits of NAN phone r = /^[0-9]\d{3}$/; result[0] = r.test(fieldValue); if(!result[0]) r = /^[0-9]$/ break; case "AreaCode": //phone numbers r = /^[2-9]\d{2}$/; result[0] = r.test(fieldValue); if(!result[0]) r = /^[0-9]$/ break; case "Phone": //phone numbers r = /^\+([- ()\+]|\d|[a-zA-Z])+$|^\(\d{3}\) \d{3}[-]\d{4}([x]\d+)?$|^\d{10,100}$/; result[0]= r.test(fieldValue); break; case "Digits": //digits only numbers r.compile("^[0-9]+$"); result[0] = r.test(fieldValue); break; case "PostalCode": //for postal code formats, numbers, letters, dashes,and spaces only r.compile("^[- a-zA-Z0-9]+$"); result[0] = r.test(fieldValue); break; case "Email": //for email alphanum, @, . //r = /^([éàèùâêîôûëïöüæçœÉÀÈÙÂÊÎÔÛËÏÖÜÆÇŒa-zA-Z0-9_\.-]+)@([\déàèùâêîôûëïöüæçœÉÀÈÙÂÊÎÔÛËÏÖÜÆÇŒa-zA-Z0-9\.-]+)\.([éàèùâêîôûëïöüæçœÉÀÈÙÂÊÎÔÛËÏÖÜÆÇŒa-zA-Z\.]{2,6})$/ //removed \. from regExpression ticket 385190 //r = /^([éàèùâêîôûëïöüæçœÉÀÈÙÂÊÎÔÛËÏÖÜÆÇŒa-zA-Z0-9_\.-]+)@([\déàèùâêîôûëïöüæçœÉÀÈÙÂÊÎÔÛËÏÖÜÆÇŒa-zA-Z0-9\.-]+)\.([éàèùâêîôûëïöüæçœÉÀÈÙÂÊÎÔÛËÏÖÜÆÇŒa-zA-Z]{2,6})$/ //r = /^[a-zA-Z0-9_\-éàèùâêîôûäëïöüæçœÉÀÈÙÂÊÎÔÛÄËÏÖÜÆÇŒ]+(?:\.[a-zA-Z0-9_\-éàèùâêîôûäëïöüæçœÉÀÈÙÂÊÎÔÛÄËÏÖÜÆÇŒ]+)*@[a-zA-Z0-9]+(?:\.[a-zA-Z0-9]+)*\.[a-zA-Z]{2,6}$/ //Updated 10/2025 - Bug 1091189 r = /^[A-Za-z0-9_\-éàèùâêîôûäëïöüæçœÉÀÈÙÂÊÎÔÛÄËÏÖÜÆÇŒ]+(\.[A-Za-z0-9_\-éàèùâêîôûäëïöüæçœÉÀÈÙÂÊÎÔÛÄËÏÖÜÆÇŒ]+)*@[A-Za-z0-9]+(-[A-Za-z0-9]+)*(\.[A-Za-z0-9]+(-[A-Za-z0-9]+)*)*\.[A-Za-z]{2,6}$/ //Updated
